- **fight**: You MUST use this to run substantial work in this repo's workflow. The goal is to be the single coordinator (chiefmate): own the sequence Brainstorm -&gt; Roadmap -&gt; Design -&gt; Plan -&gt; Development (loop) -&gt; Deploy, the gates, subagent dispatch, and the loop state. You are not allowed to skip a gate or the loop. Skip it for a question, reading code, or a trivial fix. Trigger phrases: fight mode, /fight, run the workflow. ([sghoang24/toothless](https://github.com/sghoang24/toothless/tree/HEAD/skills/fight))
